Operational Hallucination and Safety Drift in AI Agents
Authors: Shasha Yu, Fiona Carroll, Barry L. Bentley
First: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) serving as planners in tool-using autonomous agents introduce dynamic reliability risks in multi-turn execution. While single-turn safety mechanisms are relatively mature, extended interactions reveal structural vulnerabilities where initial alignment degrades over time. This paper empirically characterizes two observed failure modes across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: Safety Drift, the gradual erosion of declared safety intent leading to constraint-violating actions (e.g., textual refusal followed by reconnaissance and unsafe execution), and Operational Hallucination, persistent repetitive tool calls indicative of flawed state perception (e.g., livelocks even in legitimate tasks). Through controlled multi-turn evaluation on high-stakes ethical dilemmas, malicious requests, and benign controls, we quantify these phenomena using declaration-action gap and livelock metrics, demonstrating their cross-model prevalence under direct execution protocols. Root-cause analysis attributes the instabilities to the decoupling of reasoning context from execution state in current agent loops. We propose an Action-Aware Supervision Layer - a lightweight, plug-and-play architectural blueprint incorporating intent-action consistency checks, runtime state tracking, and forced termination primitives. Post-hoc simulation on captured failure trajectories shows the layer can intercept observed violations without false positives on benign cases. This work advances agent reliability by shifting focus from linguistic safeguards to enforceable architectural mechanisms for responsible agentic AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能代理中的操作性幻觉与安全漂移
作为工具使用自主代理的规划者，大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮执行中引入了动态可靠性风险。虽然单轮安全机制相对成熟，但扩展交互揭示了结构性脆弱性，初始对齐随着时间的推移而下降。本文实证描述了多个最先进LLMs中观察到的两种失败模式：安全漂移，即声明的安全意图逐渐侵蚀，导致违反约束的行为（例如，文本拒绝后进行侦察和不安全执行）；操作性幻觉，即持续重复的工具调用，表明状态感知存在缺陷（例如，即使在合法任务中也出现活锁）。通过对高风险伦理困境、恶意请求和良性控制的受控多轮评估，我们使用声明-行动差距和活锁指标量化这些现象，展示它们在直接执行协议下的跨模型普遍性。根本原因分析将不稳定性归因于当前代理循环中推理上下文与执行状态的解耦。我们提出了一种行动感知监督层——一种轻量级、即插即用的架构蓝图，包含意图-行动一致性检查、运行时状态跟踪和强制终止原语。对捕获的失败轨迹进行的事后模拟表明，该层可以在良性案例中拦截观察到的违规行为而不产生假阳性。该工作通过将重点从语言保障转向可执行的架构机制，推动了代理的可靠性，以实现负责任的代理人工智能。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the reliability risks associated with large language models (LLMs) used as planners in autonomous agents, particularly during multi-turn interactions where initial safety alignment can degrade. Previous methods primarily focused on single-turn safety mechanisms, which do not adequately address the structural vulnerabilities that arise over extended interactions, leading to issues like Safety Drift and Operational Hallucination. This paper contributes by empirically characterizing these failure modes and proposing an Action-Aware Supervision Layer that enhances agent reliability through intent-action consistency checks and runtime state tracking. The methodology involves controlled multi-turn evaluations on ethical dilemmas and malicious requests, revealing that the proposed layer can effectively intercept violations without generating false positives in benign scenarios, thus supporting the goal of improving safety in AI agents.
本研究关注于作为规划者的语言模型（LLMs）在自主代理中的可靠性风险，特别是在多轮交互中，初始安全对齐可能会下降。以往的方法主要集中在单轮安全机制上，未能充分解决在扩展交互中出现的结构性脆弱性，导致安全漂移和操作性幻觉等问题。本文通过实证研究这些失败模式，并提出了一种行动感知监督层，增强了通过意图-行动一致性检查和运行时状态跟踪来提高可靠性。该方法涉及对伦理困境和其他场景的受控多轮评估，证明所提层能够有效拦截违规行为而不产生误报，从而支持提高复杂任务中代理安全性和可靠性的目标。
Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. Most safety benchmarks evaluate defenders against fixed attack pools collected before evaluation, single-turn or multi-turn. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for \emph{adaptive multi-round attacks against memoryless LLM defenders}: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. Holding the 21 scenarios, attackers, defenders, and structured-output scoring fixed, restricting scoring to the first attacker turn yields $0$-$1\%$ attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds of adaptive attack yields $5.4$-$14.0\%$. Pooling three frontier attacker LLMs uncovers $1.4$-$2.2\times$ as many unique successful attacks as the best single attacker, and the generated attacks have low cosine similarity ($0.02$-$0.14$) to attacks in existing benchmarks. Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4 are tied in aggregate ($5.4\%$ each; overlapping $95\%$ CIs), but their weaknesses differ sharply: on one scenario Opus reaches $60\%$ ASR ($95\%$ CI $36$--$80\%$) while GPT-5.4 and Gemini each stay at $7\%$ (CI $1$-$30\%$; the gap is preserved in a higher-$N$ replication). $13$ of $21$ scenarios distinguish at least one defender pair, yet rankings disagree across scenarios (Kendall's $W = 0.19$). We release the benchmark -- 21 evaluation scenarios, 10 public development scenarios, the orchestrator, baseline harnesses, and a multi-attacker CLI -- plus 945 transcripts from the 3$\times$3 frontier matrix, an attack-replay dataset, and 18{,}422 gpt-oss-20b battles from an open competition's final scoring rounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。大多数安全基准测试评估防御者对固定攻击池的抵御能力，这些攻击池在评估前收集，分为单回合或多回合。我们提出了一个21场景的基准测试，用于\emph{针对无记忆LLM防御者的自适应多轮攻击}：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。在保持21个场景、攻击者、防御者和结构化输出评分固定的情况下，将评分限制在第一个攻击者回合，攻击成功率（ASR）为$0$-$1\%$；允许15轮自适应攻击则产生$5.4$-$14.0\%$的成功率。汇聚三种前沿攻击者LLM发现其独特成功攻击数量是最佳单一攻击者的$1.4$-$2.2$倍，且生成的攻击与现有基准中的攻击的余弦相似度较低（$0.02$-$0.14$）。Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.4在总计上相同（各为$5.4\%$；重叠$95\%$置信区间），但它们的弱点差异明显：在一个场景中，Opus达到$60\%$的ASR（$95\%$ CI $36$--$80\%$），而GPT-5.4和Gemini各自保持在$7\%$（CI $1$-$30\%$；在更高的$N$复制中保持差距）。$21$个场景中有$13$个区分至少一对防御者，但场景间的排名不一致（Kendall's $W = 0.19$）。我们发布了基准测试——21个评估场景、10个公共开发场景、协调器、基线工具和多攻击者CLI——以及来自3$\times$3前沿矩阵的945个转录、一个攻击重放数据集和来自开放竞赛最终评分回合的18,422场gpt-oss-20b对战。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the vulnerability of LLM-based agents to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation, highlighting the limitations of existing safety benchmarks that typically evaluate against fixed attack scenarios and do not account for adaptive attacks. The proposed approach introduces a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive multi-round attacks, where an autonomous LLM attacker can observe and adjust its strategy based on the defender's previous responses, thereby creating a more dynamic evaluation environment. The contribution lies in demonstrating that allowing for adaptive attacks significantly increases the attack success rate, with findings showing that the best-performing attackers can achieve a 5.4% to 14.0% attack success rate compared to 0-1% in fixed scenarios. The methodology involves a structured evaluation of both attackers and defenders across multiple rounds, revealing distinct weaknesses among different LLM defenders and providing a comprehensive dataset for future research in LLM security.
本研究关注基于LLM的智能体在提示注入和多轮操控方面的脆弱性，强调了现有安全基准的局限性，这些基准通常在单轮或多轮场景中对固定攻击池进行评估。所提出的方法引入了一个21场景的基准，用于自适应多轮攻击，其中自主LLM攻击者可以根据先前防御者的响应调整其策略，从而提供对LLM防御者的更动态评估。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它揭示了静态评估的不足，表明允许自适应攻击显著提高了攻击成功率，从0-1%提高到5.4-14%。本文的贡献在于发布了一个全面的基准，包括评估场景和数据集，并显示多个先进攻击者的组合可以比单一攻击者产生更多独特的成功攻击，不同LLM（如Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.4）在特定场景中的攻击成功率最高可达60%。
STAC: When Innocent Tools Form Dangerous Chains for LLM Agents
Authors: Jing-Jing Li, Jianfeng He, Chao Shang, Devang Kulshreshtha, Xun Xian, Yi Zhang, Hang Su, Sandesh Swamy, Yanjun Qi
First: 2025-09-30T00:31:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-19T03:06:33+00:00
Comments: Long version with 15 pages in main draft and a total of 39 pages including references and appendix. Data and code \url{https://github.com/amazon-science/MultiTurnAgentAttack}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As LLMs advance into autonomous agents with tool-use capabilities, they introduce security challenges that extend beyond traditional content-based LLM safety concerns. This paper introduces Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (\STAC), a novel multi-turn attack framework that exploits agent tool use. \STAC chains together tool calls that each appear harmless in isolation but, when combined, collectively enable harmful operations that only become apparent at the final execution step. At the core of \STAC is an automated, closed-loop pipeline that synthesizes executable multi-step tool chains, validates them through in-environment execution, and reverse-engineers stealthy multi-turn prompts that reliably induce agents to execute the verified malicious sequence. Using this framework, we generate and systematically evaluate 483 \STAC cases, featuring 1,352 sets of user-agent-environment interactions and spanning diverse domains, tasks, agent types, and 10 failure modes. Our evaluations show that state-of-the-art LLM agents are highly vulnerable to \STAC, with an average final attack success rate (ASR) of 91.2\% -- exceeding 90\% for all but one of the eight agents evaluated. We further perform defense analysis and find that existing prompt-based defenses provide limited protection. To address this gap, we propose a new reasoning-driven defense prompt that achieves the strongest initial-turn protection, cutting ASR by up to 28.8\%; however, this advantage erodes sharply under adaptive attacks, and an experience-based defense (ToolShield) proves more durable over sustained multi-turn interactions. These results highlight a crucial gap: defending tool-enabled agents requires reasoning over entire action sequences and their cumulative effects, rather than evaluating isolated prompts or responses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STAC：当无辜工具形成对LLM代理的危险链条
随着LLM向具备工具使用能力的自主代理发展，它们引入了超越传统内容基础LLM安全问题的安全挑战。本文介绍了顺序工具攻击链（STAC），一种利用代理工具使用的新型多轮攻击框架。STAC将看似无害的工具调用串联在一起，但当组合时，集体启用的有害操作仅在最终执行步骤时显现。STAC的核心是一个自动化的闭环管道，合成可执行的多步骤工具链，通过环境执行验证，并逆向工程隐秘的多轮提示，可靠地诱导代理执行经过验证的恶意序列。利用该框架，我们生成并系统评估了483个STAC案例，涉及1,352组用户-代理-环境交互，涵盖多种领域、任务、代理类型和10种失败模式。我们的评估显示，最先进的LLM代理对STAC高度脆弱，平均最终攻击成功率（ASR）为91.2%，除了评估的八个代理中有一个外，其他均超过90%。我们进一步进行防御分析，发现现有的基于提示的防御提供的保护有限。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的基于推理的防御提示，达到了最强的初始回合保护，将ASR降低了多达28.8%；然而，在自适应攻击下，这一优势迅速减弱，而基于经验的防御（ToolShield）在持续的多轮交互中表现得更为持久。这些结果突显了一个关键差距：防御工具启用的代理需要对整个行动序列及其累积效果进行推理，而不是评估孤立的提示或响应。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the emerging security challenges posed by large language models (LLMs) as they evolve into autonomous agents capable of tool use, which extends beyond traditional safety concerns. Previous methods primarily focused on content-based safety, failing to account for the risks associated with multi-turn interactions and tool chaining. The proposed Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (STAC) framework differs by systematically exploiting seemingly harmless tool calls that, when combined, enable harmful operations. This approach is well-motivated as it reveals vulnerabilities in state-of-the-art LLM agents, demonstrated through the generation and evaluation of 483 STAC cases across various domains, achieving an average attack success rate of 91.2%. The paper contributes by highlighting the inadequacy of existing defenses and proposing a new reasoning-driven defense prompt that reduces attack success rates, although it notes that more robust defenses like ToolShield are necessary for sustained protection against adaptive attacks.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）作为具备工具使用能力的自主代理所带来的新兴安全挑战，这些挑战超出了传统安全问题的范围。以往的方法主要集中于基于内容的安全性，未能考虑多轮交互和工具链的风险。提出的顺序工具攻击链（STAC）框架创新性地利用这些交互，通过创建自动化的可执行多步骤工具链，这些工具链在单独使用时看似无害，但结合后可能导致有害结果。本文的贡献在于系统评估了483个STAC案例，揭示了最先进的LLM代理的平均攻击成功率为91.2%。此外，本文还引入了一种新的基于推理的防御提示，显著降低了攻击成功率，尽管在自适应攻击下其有效性减弱，而基于经验的防御（ToolShield）在持续交互中提供了更强的保护。
Addressing Benchmarking Gaps in Large Language Models for Health and Medicine with Dynamic Red-Teaming
Authors: Jiazhen Pan, Bailiang Jian, Paul Hager, Yundi Zhang, Che Liu, Friederike Jungmann, Hongwei Bran Li, Julian Canisius, Chenyu You, Junde Wu, Jiayuan Zhu, Fenglin Liu, Yuyuan Liu, Niklas Bubeck, Moritz Knolle, Chen, Chen, Christian Wachinger, Zhenyu Gong, Cheng Ouyang, Georgios Kaissis, Benedikt Wiestler, Daniel Rueckert
First: 2025-07-30T08:44:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:41:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer health-related questions and support healthcare workflows, yet evidence for their safety still relies heavily on static benchmarks that can rapidly become obsolete or be optimized against. Here we introduce a Dynamic, Automatic, and Systematic (DAS) red-teaming audit framework that continuously stress-tests LLMs for health across four safety-critical axes: robustness, privacy, bias/fairness, and hallucination/factual inaccuracies. Validated against board-certified clinicians with high concordance, a suite of adversarial agents autonomously mutates health-related test cases to uncover vulnerabilities in real time. Applying DAS to 15 proprietary and open-source LLMs revealed a profound gap between high static benchmark performance and low dynamic reliability--the "Benchmarking Gap". Despite median MedQA accuracy exceeding 80\%, 94\% of previously correct answers failed under dynamic robustness testing. This brittleness generalized to the realistic, open-ended HealthBench dataset, where top-tier models exhibited failure rates exceeding 70\% and sharp shifts in model rankings across evaluations, suggesting that high scores on established static benchmarks may reflect superficial memorization. We observed similarly high failure rates across other domains: privacy leaks were elicited in 86\% of scenarios, cognitive-bias priming altered recommendations in 81\% of fairness tests, and hallucination rates exceeded 74\% in widely used models. By converting LLM safety evaluation for health from a static checklist into a living adversarial audit, DAS provides a scalable framework for surfacing latent risks before such systems are deployed in consumer-facing health assistants, clinician-facing tools, and broader healthcare workflows. Code is available at https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动态红队解决大型语言模型在健康和医学中的基准测试差距
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于回答与健康相关的问题和支持医疗工作流程，但其安全性的证据仍然在很大程度上依赖于快速过时或可优化的静态基准。在这里，我们引入了一种动态、自动和系统化（DAS）红队审计框架，持续对LLMs在健康方面进行压力测试，涵盖四个安全关键轴心：鲁棒性、隐私、公平性/偏见和幻觉/事实不准确性。经过与高一致性的董事会认证临床医生的验证，一套对抗性代理自主变更与健康相关的测试案例，以实时发现漏洞。将DAS应用于15个专有和开源LLMs揭示了高静态基准性能与低动态可靠性之间的深刻差距——“基准测试差距”。尽管中位数MedQA准确率超过80%，94%的先前正确答案在动态鲁棒性测试中失败。这种脆弱性在现实的开放式HealthBench数据集中普遍存在，顶级模型的失败率超过70%，并且在评估中模型排名发生剧烈变化，表明在既定静态基准上获得的高分可能反映了表面的记忆。我们在其他领域观察到类似的高失败率：在86%的场景中引发了隐私泄露，81%的公平性测试中认知偏见的引导改变了推荐，广泛使用的模型中幻觉率超过74%。通过将LLM安全评估从静态检查表转变为动态对抗审计，DAS提供了一个可扩展的框架，以在这些系统部署到面向消费者的健康助手、面向临床医生的工具和更广泛的医疗工作流程之前，揭示潜在风险。代码可在https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the increasing reliance on large language models (LLMs) in healthcare, highlighting the limitations of static benchmarks that may quickly become outdated or manipulated. The Joint Effect of Quantization and Sampling Temperature on LLM Safety Alignment: A Factorial Analysis
Authors: Hari Prasad, Ritam Pal
First: 2026-06-28T19:52:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T10:24:21+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM deployments often combine quantization with higher sampling temperatures to reduce cost, latency, or repetition, yet safety evaluations usually treat these as fixed implementation details. We test whether models that are safe at FP16 with greedy decoding remain safe after quantization and stochastic sampling, or whether the two factors amplify each other. We evaluate 8 instruction-tuned models from five families across 3 precisions and 6 temperatures, covering 144 configurations on 7 harmfulness benchmarks and generating about 2.0 million responses, which are scored by a six-judge safety ensemble. Contrary to concerns that low-bit deployment erodes alignment, we find that standard quantization is approximately safety-neutral: for 7 of 8 models, AWQ INT4 keeps attack success within about 1.6 percentage points of FP16 or lowers it, with clear degradation only for SmolLM3-3B (34.5% to 44.1%). However, the larger risk comes from sampling: higher temperatures sharply increase decision instability, with DFR reaching 41.9% at T = 1.0, even when average ASR changes only modestly. The two factors do not compound: our Compound Degradation Index remains sub-additive (-0.071 to +0.018), indicating that quantization partially offsets rather than amplifies temperature-induced degradation. Finally, a per-benchmark breakdown shows that single-benchmark evaluation badly understates risk: several models scoring 0% on AdvBench exceed 80% on ManyHarm. Standard INT4/INT8 quantization can therefore be reasonable for well-aligned models, but safety claims should report multi-sample stability across multiple benchmarks rather than rely on a single benchmark at greedy decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化与采样温度对大型语言模型安全对齐的联合影响：一个因子分析
现代大型语言模型的部署通常将量化与更高的采样温度结合，以降低成本、延迟或重复性，但安全评估通常将这些视为固定的实施细节。我们测试在FP16下使用贪婪解码安全的模型在量化和随机采样后是否仍然安全，或者这两个因素是否相互放大。我们评估了来自五个家族的8个指令调优模型，涵盖3种精度和6种温度，共144种配置，在7个有害性基准上生成约200万条响应，并由六名评审组成的安全团队进行评分。与低位部署会削弱对齐的担忧相反，我们发现标准量化大致是安全中性的：对于8个模型中的7个，AWQ INT4将攻击成功率保持在FP16的约1.6个百分点内或降低，只有SmolLM3-3B出现明显退化（34.5%降至44.1%）。然而，较大的风险来自采样：更高的温度显著增加决策不稳定性，在T = 1.0时DFR达到41.9%，即使平均ASR变化仅适度。这两个因素并不相互叠加：我们的复合退化指数保持在亚加性范围内（-0.071至+0.018），表明量化部分抵消而非放大温度引起的退化。最后，逐基准的细分显示，单基准评估严重低估了风险：几个在AdvBench上得分为0%的模型在ManyHarm上超过80%。因此，标准INT4/INT8量化对于对齐良好的模型是合理的，但安全声明应报告多个基准的多样本稳定性，而不是依赖于贪婪解码的单一基准。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) when subjected to quantization and varying sampling temperatures, which are commonly used to optimize performance but often overlooked in safety evaluations. Previous methods typically treated quantization and sampling as fixed parameters, raising concerns about their potential negative impact on model safety. The proposed approach systematically evaluates the interaction between these factors across multiple configurations and benchmarks, revealing that standard quantization is generally safety-neutral while higher sampling temperatures significantly increase decision instability. The study contributes by demonstrating that quantization does not exacerbate safety risks and that a comprehensive evaluation across multiple benchmarks is essential for accurate safety assessments. The methodology involves testing eight instruction-tuned models across three precisions and six temperatures, generating approximately 2 million responses, and assessing them with a safety ensemble, ultimately showing that while quantization can be safe for aligned models, the effects of sampling temperature must be carefully monitored to ensure safety alignment.
本研究探讨了在现代大语言模型（LLM）部署中，量化和高采样温度对模型安全对齐的影响。以往的方法通常将量化和采样视为固定参数，而未评估它们对模型安全性的综合影响，这导致了对对齐可能下降的担忧。所提出的方法系统地评估了量化和采样温度在多个模型和配置之间的相互作用，结果表明，尽管量化通常是安全中性的，但较高的采样温度显著增加了决策的不稳定性。本文的贡献在于证明量化不会加剧与采样相关的风险，并强调多基准评估在准确安全评估中的重要性。该方法论涉及对8个指令调优模型的144个配置进行因子分析，得出的见解表明，对于对齐良好的模型，标准量化是可接受的，前提是安全评估考虑多个基准的稳定性，而不是依赖单一指标。
Minionese: Comprehensive Benchmark and Mechanistic Study of Multilingual LLM Safety
Authors: Chigozirim Ifebi, Brent Kong, Ayushi Mehrotra
First: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models remains brittle across languages: prompts reliably refused in English can elicit harmful compliance in non-English and low-resource settings. We introduce \textsc{Minionese}, a multilingual jailbreak benchmark spanning 18 languages, 4 resource tiers, and 4 perturbation types (standard translation, code-switching, transliteration, and translationese), paired with a geometric mechanistic analysis of refusal failure across language tiers. We show that each attack type produces a distinct vulnerability profile: transliteration vulnerability is mediated by script identity, code-switching maintains effectiveness through the lowest-resource tier, and a sharp safety regime transition between Tiers 2 and 3 is consistent across all models. Mechanistically, low-resource jailbreaks succeed by routing harmful content through a geometrically misaligned subspace that projects insufficiently onto the refusal directions, leaving the refusal mechanism intact but untriggered. These findings show that English-only safety evaluations are insufficient; they require accounting for script family, perturbation type, and per-language alignment coverage. The benchmark and analysis code is at https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小黄人语：多语言大型语言模型安全性的综合基准和机制研究
大型语言模型的安全对齐在不同语言间仍然脆弱：在英语中可靠拒绝的提示在非英语和低资源环境中可能引发有害的顺从。我们介绍了\textsc{Minionese}，这是一个涵盖18种语言、4个资源层级和4种扰动类型（标准翻译、代码切换、音译和翻译语）的多语言越狱基准，并结合了对不同语言层级拒绝失败的几何机制分析。我们展示了每种攻击类型产生独特的脆弱性特征：音译脆弱性受脚本身份的影响，代码切换在最低资源层级中保持有效，而第2层和第3层之间的安全机制转变在所有模型中是一致的。从机制上讲，低资源越狱通过将有害内容引导到几何上不对齐的子空间中成功，该子空间在拒绝方向上投影不足，保持拒绝机制完好但未被触发。这些发现表明，仅依赖英语的安全评估是不够的；它们需要考虑脚本家族、扰动类型和每种语言的对齐覆盖。基准和分析代码可在https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git找到。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the issue of safety alignment in large language models, which has been shown to be inconsistent across different languages, particularly where prompts that are refused in English may lead to harmful outputs in non-English contexts. Previous methods primarily focused on English, failing to account for the complexities of multilingual environments, which this study aims to rectify by introducing the Minionese benchmark. This benchmark evaluates multilingual jailbreak vulnerabilities across 18 languages and various resource tiers, revealing distinct vulnerability profiles for different attack types. The proposed methodology includes a geometric mechanistic analysis that identifies how low-resource jailbreaks exploit misaligned subspaces, leading to insufficient triggering of refusal mechanisms. The findings indicate that existing safety evaluations are inadequate and highlight the necessity for a more comprehensive approach that considers language-specific factors, ultimately demonstrating the benchmark's effectiveness in revealing vulnerabilities that could compromise safety in multilingual settings.
本研究关注大型语言模型的安全对齐问题，该问题在不同语言中往往不一致，导致在非英语和低资源环境中产生有害输出。以往的方法主要集中在英语上，未能考虑多语言的脆弱性，而本研究旨在通过引入Minionese，一个评估18种语言及各种扰动类型的多语言越狱基准，来纠正这一点。本文的贡献在于全面分析不同攻击类型如何揭示独特的脆弱性，表明音译和代码切换可以利用安全机制中的弱点。该方法论涉及几何机制分析，以理解拒绝失败，揭示低资源越狱通过利用不对齐的子空间来成功，从而强调了以英语为中心的评估的不足。研究结果强调了多语言安全评估的必要性，表明所提出的基准有效识别脆弱性，并支持增强不同语言环境下语言模型安全性的目标。
Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs
Authors: Anupam Wagle, Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin, Chaowei Zhang, Longwei Wang
First: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过内部归因图对LLM越狱的机制性可解释性
大型语言模型（LLMs）展现出显著的能力，但仍然对对抗性提示和越狱攻击高度脆弱。现有方法主要通过输入输出行为或归因方法分析这些失败，提供的见解有限，无法深入理解对抗性扰动如何改变模型的内部推理。因此，导致不安全或不正确行为的机制仍然不清楚。我们引入了一种机制框架，通过配对的内部计算图来诊断LLM的脆弱性，这些图表示特定提示的推理为潜在特征之间的结构性因果交互。通过构建和对齐干净和攻击提示的计算图，我们揭示了对抗性攻击引发的内部推理的系统性转变，包括安全相关组件的抑制、攻击特定特征的出现和计算路径的重新引导。在此表示的基础上，我们提出了一个统一框架，(i) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和新兴结构，(ii) 识别与失败模式相关的重复脆弱性特征，(iii) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预，以直接评估它们对攻击成功的贡献。这使得从描述性归因转向模型失败的因果诊断。针对多个开源LLM和多样的对抗性及越狱基准的实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为强烈相关。此外，对识别出的脆弱性特征进行有针对性的干预提高了模型的鲁棒性，确立了内部计算图作为理解、诊断和减轻LLM脆弱性的原则基础。

Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment
Authors: Marcus Williams, Hannah Sheahan, Cameron Raymond, Tomek Korbak, Deng Pan, Peilin Yang, Leon Maksin, Ningyi Xie, Phillip Guo, Ian Kivlichan, Micah Carroll
First: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00
Comments: 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.

Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety
Authors: Lifei Liu, Haoran Yu, Xiaochong Jiang, Su Wang, Pin Qian, Yihang Chen
First: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance. Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance. Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

标题：多智能体LLM安全中的操作重构与批准框架委托
多智能体LLM系统的安全评估通常将直接提示与规划-执行管道进行比较，并将差异报告为单一的“管道效应”。我们认为，这种汇总难以解释，因为它混淆了三种机制：有害意图可能被重构为合理的操作工作，规划者可能拒绝或转变请求，而执行者可能在暗示先前批准的委托提示下行动。为了分离这些因素，我们引入了一个五条件的对照设计，在30个合成有害场景和来自四个代理安全基准的探索性外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM判断的合规性。我们的结果表明，汇总管道安全并不是一个稳定的架构属性。操作重构是最具可移植性的风险信号，在两个场景集上提高了GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek的合规性，而Claude则相对抗拒。规划者的行为主要通过拒绝来抵消这一风险；然而，当规划者产生可执行步骤时，执行者的合规性可能比直接操作基线更高。批准框架委托对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感，而怀疑的执行者提示会显著降低合规性。原始直接模型排名也可能错误预测已部署的规划-执行行为。在主要集中的原始直接提示下，Gemini是最安全的，但在Claude规划者下显示出最大的放大，从8.9%上升到38.9%的合规性。GPT的近零汇总管道效应则掩盖了被规划者拒绝抵消的重构增加。这些发现表明，多智能体安全评估应分别报告重构、规划者行为、委托框架和模型配对，然后再将失败归因于架构本身。

Previous methods have conflated harmful intent, planner behavior, and executor responses, leading to misinterpretations of safety. The proposed approach introduces a controlled contrast design that isolates these factors across various scenarios, providing a clearer understanding of compliance dynamics. DT-Guard: Intent-Driven Reasoning-Active Training for Reasoning-Free LLM Safety Guardrail
Authors: He Liu, Changtao Miao, Xinjie Yang, Tianle Song, Yin Wu, Junchi Chen, Bintao He, Xinyuan Zhang, Bo Zhang, Shi Yan, Wei Lu, Wei Wang, Danyang Xu, Jiansheng Cai, Zhe Li
First: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models deployed in open-world applications require safety guardrails that are both robust to complex risks and efficient enough for low-latency runtime moderation. Existing guardrails face a practical trade-off between lightweight classification-based models, which are efficient but often struggle with concealed intent, ambiguous semantics, and borderline safety decisions, and reasoning-based guards, which improve judgment quality but introduce additional token generation and inference latency. We present DT-Guard, a content safety guardrail model based on a Reasoning-Active Training, Reasoning-Free Inference paradigm. The key idea is to use reasoning supervision during training while emitting only structured safety labels at inference time. DT-Guard formulates safety judgment as a progressive decision process, Intent - Category - Safety, and constructs an intent-driven dataset with intent labels, risk categories, safety labels, and structured reasoning trajectories. To further improve hard-case robustness, we propose Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization (RG-PHO), which uses multi-rollout consistency to identify stably mastered, persistently failed, and preference-unstable samples, and applies targeted supervised and preference optimization accordingly. At inference time, DT-Guard directly generates structured labels without explicit reasoning traces, preserving deployment efficiency. Experiments on prompt-side and response-side safety benchmarks show that DT-Guard achieves average F1 scores of 0.886 and 0.870, respectively. With only a 4B backbone, it reaches a dual-side average F1 of 0.878, outperforming strong 8B guardrail baselines. These results demonstrate that reasoning supervision can be effectively internalized into low-latency safety discrimination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DT-Guard：基于意图驱动推理主动训练的无推理大型语言模型安全护栏
在开放世界应用中部署的大型语言模型需要既能应对复杂风险又能高效低延迟的安全护栏。现有护栏在轻量级基于分类的模型（高效但常常难以处理隐含意图、模糊语义和边界安全决策）与基于推理的护栏（提高判断质量但引入额外的令牌生成和推理延迟）之间面临实际权衡。我们提出了DT-Guard，一种基于推理主动训练、无推理推断范式的内容安全护栏模型。其关键思想是在训练期间使用推理监督，而在推断时仅发出结构化安全标签。DT-Guard将安全判断公式化为一个渐进决策过程：意图 - 类别 - 安全，并构建了一个带有意图标签、风险类别、安全标签和结构化推理轨迹的意图驱动数据集。为了进一步提高难例的鲁棒性，我们提出了回滚引导渐进难例优化（RG-PHO），利用多回滚一致性识别稳定掌握、持续失败和偏好不稳定的样本，并相应地应用有针对性的监督和偏好优化。在推断时，DT-Guard直接生成结构化标签，而无需显式的推理痕迹，从而保持部署效率。在提示端和响应端的安全基准测试中，DT-Guard分别达到了平均F1分数0.886和0.870。仅使用4B主干，它的双侧平均F1达到了0.878，超越了强大的8B护栏基线。这些结果表明，推理监督可以有效地内化为低延迟的安全判别。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for effective safety guardrails in large language models (LLMs) used in open-world applications, highlighting the limitations of existing methods that either compromise efficiency or struggle with complex safety judgments. Previous approaches often rely on lightweight classification models that fail to capture concealed intent and ambiguous semantics, while reasoning-based models introduce latency issues. The proposed DT-Guard model innovatively combines reasoning supervision during training with reasoning-free inference, allowing for efficient safety judgments through a structured decision-making process. This paper contributes a novel intent-driven dataset and introduces Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization (RG-PHO) to enhance robustness against challenging cases. Experimental results demonstrate that DT-Guard achieves competitive performance with average F1 scores of 0.886 and 0.870 on safety benchmarks, surpassing existing 8B guardrail models with a 4B backbone, thus validating the effectiveness of reasoning supervision in maintaining low-latency safety discrimination.

Chasing Moving Targets with Online Self-Play Reinforcement Learning for Safer Language Models
Authors: Mickel Liu, Liwei Jiang, Yancheng Liang, Simon Shaolei Du, Yejin Choi, Tim Althoff, Natasha Jaques
Venue: ICML 2026 Poster
First: 2025-06-09T06:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T07:31:35+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Poster
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conventional large language model (LLM) safety alignment relies on a reactive, disjoint loop: attackers exploit a static model, then defenders patch exposed vulnerabilities. This sequential setup leads to attackers overfitting obsolete exploits while defenders perpetually lag behind emerging threats. To address this, we introduce Self-RedTeam, the first fully online self-play multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that continuously co-evolves attacker and defender for robust safety alignment. A single policy self-plays as both attacker and defender, generating adversarial prompts and defending against them, with a reward model adjudicating outcomes. Each role uses hidden chain-of-thought for strategic planning. Grounded in two-player zero-sum game theory, we establish a theoretical safety guarantee: if the game converges to Nash Equilibrium, the defender produces safe responses against any adversarial input. Empirically, Self-RedTeam generalizes across five models from the Llama and Qwen families, uncovering more diverse attacks (+17.80% SBERT) and improving safety of RLHF-trained models by up to 95% across 14 benchmarks. Our work motivates a shift from reactive patching to proactive co-evolution, enabling LLM safety self-improvement via online self-play MARL. Link to code: https://github.com/mickelliu/selfplay-redteaming
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过在线自我对弈强化学习追踪移动目标以实现更安全的语言模型
传统的大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐依赖于反应性、分离的循环：攻击者利用静态模型，防御者修补暴露的漏洞。这种顺序设置导致攻击者过度拟合过时的攻击，而防御者则始终滞后于新兴威胁。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了Self-RedTeam，这是第一个完全在线自我对弈的多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法，持续共同进化攻击者和防御者以实现稳健的安全对齐。单一策略同时扮演攻击者和防御者，生成对抗性提示并进行防御，奖励模型裁定结果。每个角色使用隐含的思维链进行战略规划。基于双人零和博弈理论，我们建立了理论安全保证：如果游戏收敛到纳什均衡，防御者将对任何对抗性输入产生安全响应。在经验上，Self-RedTeam在Llama和Qwen家族的五个模型中具有广泛的泛化能力，发现了更多样化的攻击（+17.80% SBERT），并在14个基准测试中将RLHF训练模型的安全性提高了多达95%。我们的工作激励从反应性修补转向主动共同进化，使LLM安全性通过在线自我对弈MARL实现自我改进。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of conventional safety alignment in large language models (LLMs), which typically follow a reactive approach where defenders patch vulnerabilities after attackers exploit them. Past methods have resulted in a lag in defense mechanisms, allowing attackers to exploit outdated weaknesses. The proposed approach, Self-RedTeam, introduces a fully online self-play multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that enables continuous co-evolution of attackers and defenders, thus enhancing safety alignment. This method is well-motivated as it shifts from reactive to proactive strategies, allowing LLMs to self-improve their safety through ongoing adversarial interactions. The methodology involves a single policy that alternates between attacker and defender roles, utilizing hidden chain-of-thought for strategic planning and ensuring safety through a theoretical guarantee based on Nash Equilibrium. Empirical results demonstrate that Self-RedTeam generalizes across five models, revealing a 17.80% increase in attack diversity and improving the safety of RLHF-trained models by up to 95% across 14 benchmarks, supporting the goal of robust safety alignment.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐的局限性，传统方法通常采用反应式的方式，防御者在攻击者利用漏洞后进行修补。这种方法导致防御机制滞后于新兴威胁。提出的方法Self-RedTeam引入了一种新颖的在线自我对抗多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法，使攻击者和防御者能够持续共同进化，从而增强安全对齐。这种方法的动机明确，因为它从反应式转向主动策略，利用单一策略自我扮演两个角色，并采用隐含的思维链进行战略规划。本文的贡献包括基于纳什均衡建立理论安全保证，并在实证上展示安全性的改善，Self-RedTeam在14个基准测试中实现了高达95%的安全性提升，并发现比现有的Llama和Qwen系列模型多出17.80%的多样化攻击，从而支持其强健的安全对齐目标。
Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring
Authors: William Hackett, Peter Garraghan
First: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 13 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拒绝背后的原因：通过行为监测确定护栏激活
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）和自主系统逐渐融入现实应用，确保其安全性至关重要。检测并阻止发送到和从LLM传出的恶意指令的护栏系统是AI安全的重要组成部分。然而，进行黑箱对抗仿真以测试生产AI系统的研究人员常常难以确定是发生了护栏阻止还是LLM拒绝。这一区别很重要，因为绕过护栏的技术与绕过LLM安全对齐的技术可能有显著不同，并对攻击技术的选择和优化产生实质性影响。我们提出了首个黑箱护栏侦察方法，通过对HTTP、词汇和时序信号的行为监测，检测目标AI系统中护栏的存在，假设仅有黑箱访问权限且对护栏或AI系统没有先前知识。实验表明，我们的方法以100%的准确率检测护栏的存在，良性和恶意交互之间的行为差异具有统计学显著性（q < 0.001）。我们的方法进一步识别护栏设计阻止的内容类别，并在未见提示上以98%的平均F1分数区分护栏阻止和LLM拒绝。

The research addresses the critical need for safety and security in Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems as they are increasingly used in real-world applications. The proposed methodology introduces a novel black-box guardrail reconnaissance approach that utilizes behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals to detect guardrails without prior knowledge of the system. This method is well-motivated by the necessity to enhance AI security and has been shown to achieve 100% accuracy in detecting guardrail presence, as well as an average F1 score of 98% in distinguishing guardrail blocks from LLM rejections, thereby supporting its effectiveness in real-world applications.

Optimization Algorithms for Joint OFDM Waveform Design and RIS Configuration in 6G Networks: From Convex Relaxation to Foundation Models
Authors: Ahmet Kaplan
First: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint OFDM-RIS optimization for 6G is a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem covering sum-rate maximization, energy efficiency, max-min fairness, and peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR)-constrained objectives. Seventy-eight joint OFDM-RIS optimization works published between 2021 and 2026 are surveyed. No standardized benchmark exists, and cross-paper comparisons remain infeasible. This survey classifies these works into four paradigms: (I) model-based convex relaxation, (II) heuristic and metaheuristic search, (III) deep reinforcement and unsupervised learning, and (IV) emerging methods including foundation models (FM), diffusion-based generative AI, and quantum optimization. A literature synthesis of self-reported benchmarks shows that ML-based methods (Paradigm~III) report 95-99\% of model-based spectral efficiency at 10^2-10^4 x faster per-inference runtime (method-pair dependent; literature values are self-reported and exclude ML pre-training cost). A companion tutorial benchmark at N=16, N=64, and N=128 reveals a critical scaling property: GPU-based neural network inference (DDQN, PPO, graph neural network (GNN), unsupervised DL) is N-invariant, with identical runtime at N=16 and N=128, while iterative solvers (AO+SCA, PSO) scale polynomially. Energy efficiency (P2) and PAPR-constrained (P4) benchmarks are deferred to future work with standardized power models and waveform generators. Six open challenges emerge from the synthesis: the cross-paradigm benchmark deficit, real-world hardware-constrained deployment, joint waveform-RIS optimization for doubly-dispersive channels, multi-objective PAPR trade-offs, LLM safety in live network control, and diminishing returns of standalone heuristics. We specify requirements for a standardized benchmark. This study serves as a roadmap for researchers and practitioners working on joint OFDM-RIS optimization in 6G networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：6G网络中联合OFDM波形设计和RIS配置的优化算法：从凸松弛到基础模型
6G中的联合OFDM-RIS优化是一个混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，涵盖了总速率最大化、能量效率、最大-最小公平性和峰值-平均功率比（PAPR）约束目标。调查了2021年至2026年间发表的78项联合OFDM-RIS优化工作。尚无标准化基准，跨论文比较仍不可行。本调查将这些工作分类为四种范式：（I）基于模型的凸松弛，（II）启发式和元启发式搜索，（III）深度强化和无监督学习，以及（IV）新兴方法，包括基础模型（FM）、基于扩散的生成AI和量子优化。自报告基准的文献综合显示，基于机器学习的方法（范式III）在每次推理运行时间上以10^2-10^4倍的速度报告95-99%的基于模型的光谱效率（方法对比依赖；文献值为自报告且不包括机器学习预训练成本）。在N=16、N=64和N=128的伴随教程基准中揭示了一个关键的缩放特性：基于GPU的神经网络推理（DDQN、PPO、图神经网络（GNN）、无监督深度学习）是N不变的，在N=16和N=128时具有相同的运行时间，而迭代求解器（AO+SCA、PSO）则呈多项式缩放。能量效率（P2）和PAPR约束（P4）基准推迟到未来工作，需使用标准化功率模型和波形生成器。从综合中出现六个开放挑战：跨范式基准缺失、现实世界硬件受限的部署、双散射信道的联合波形-RIS优化、多目标PAPR权衡、LLM在实时网络控制中的安全性，以及独立启发式的收益递减。我们为标准化基准指定了要求。本研究为在6G网络中从事联合OFDM-RIS优化的研究人员和实践者提供了路线图。

The research addresses the optimization of joint OFDM-RIS in 6G networks, which is a complex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem aimed at maximizing sum-rate, energy efficiency, fairness, and adhering to peak-to-average power ratio constraints. Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models
Authors: Yanchen Yin, Dongqi Han, Linghui Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral presentation
First: 2026-06-26T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:00:34+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 19 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026 as an Oral presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：监狱攻击下的稳健有害特征：来自大型语言模型注意力头专业化的机制证据
监狱攻击绕过了大型语言模型的安全对齐，但其机制仍然不甚了解。我们提供证据表明，攻击并未全面消除安全特征，而是选择性地抑制特定的注意力头。我们识别出两种功能上不同的类型：集中在早期层的对抗性妥协头（ACHs），在攻击下被抑制；以及位于中层的安全对齐头（SAHs），即使在攻击成功时也能保持稳健的激活。消融研究支持ACHs的因果作用以及SAHs对稳健激活的贡献：抑制少量ACHs足以在通常拒绝的输入上诱发类似监狱攻击的行为，而移除SAHs则显著削弱中层的安全激活。标记级归因进一步表明，ACH抑制是由攻击模板标记特定驱动的，提供了攻击如何通过抑制ACH绕过拒绝决策而保持SAHs所维持的内部安全信号的机制性解释——我们称之为稳健有害特征。为了验证这种稳健性的实际意义，我们展示了仅仅读取这些持续的激活——无需任何训练——就能在强对抗稳健性下获得竞争性的整体检测性能。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs), which undermine safety alignment but whose mechanisms are not well understood. Previous methods have not adequately explained how these attacks operate, particularly in terms of their selective suppression of specific attention heads. This paper proposes a novel approach by identifying two types of attention heads: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs), which are suppressed during attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs), which retain robust activations. The study demonstrates that the suppression of ACHs can lead to jailbreak-like behavior, while the removal of SAHs weakens safety activations, thus providing a mechanistic understanding of the attacks. The methodology includes ablation studies and token-level attribution, revealing that persistent SAH activations can be leveraged for competitive detection performance against jailbreak attempts, supporting the paper's goals of enhancing LLM safety. The findings contribute to a deeper understanding of the internal mechanisms of LLMs under attack and propose a practical method for improving their robustness against such threats.

CASE-Bench: Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark for Large Language Models
Authors: Guangzhi Sun, Xiao Zhan, Shutong Feng, Philip C. Woodland, Jose Such
Venue: ICML 2025
First: 2025-01-24T21:55:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T22:17:10+00:00
Comments: 24 pages. This paper has been accepted at ICML 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values is essential for their safe deployment and widespread adoption. Current LLM safety benchmarks often focus solely on the refusal of individual problematic queries, which overlooks the importance of the context where the query occurs and may cause undesired refusal of queries under safe contexts that diminish user experience. Addressing this gap, we introduce CASE-Bench, a Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark that integrates context into safety assessments of LLMs. CASE-Bench assigns distinct, formally described contexts to categorized queries based on Contextual Integrity theory. Additionally, in contrast to previous studies which mainly rely on majority voting from just a few annotators, we recruited a sufficient number of annotators necessary to ensure the detection of statistically significant differences among the experimental conditions based on power analysis. Our extensive analysis using CASE-Bench on various open-source and commercial LLMs reveals a substantial and significant influence of context on human judgments (p<0.0001 from a z-test), underscoring the necessity of context in safety evaluations. We also identify notable mismatches between human judgments and LLM responses, particularly in commercial models within safe contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CASE-Bench：面向上下文的安全基准测试用于大型语言模型
将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐对于其安全部署和广泛采用至关重要。目前的LLM安全基准测试通常仅关注拒绝个别有问题的查询，这忽视了查询发生的上下文的重要性，可能导致在安全上下文中不必要地拒绝查询，从而降低用户体验。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了CASE-Bench，一个将上下文整合到LLM安全评估中的面向上下文的安全基准测试。CASE-Bench根据上下文完整性理论为分类查询分配独特的、正式描述的上下文。此外，与以往主要依赖少数注释者的多数投票的研究不同，我们招募了足够数量的注释者，以确保根据功效分析检测实验条件之间的统计显著差异。我们对各种开源和商业LLM使用CASE-Bench进行的广泛分析揭示了上下文对人类判断的显著影响（p<0.0001，来自z检验），强调了上下文在安全评估中的必要性。我们还发现人类判断与LLM响应之间存在显著不匹配，特别是在安全上下文中的商业模型。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values to ensure their safe deployment, highlighting that existing safety benchmarks often neglect the contextual factors influencing query responses. Previous methods primarily focused on individual problematic queries and relied on limited annotator input, which could lead to inadequate safety assessments. The proposed CASE-Bench introduces a Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark that incorporates contextual elements into safety evaluations, utilizing a robust number of annotators to detect statistically significant differences in responses. This methodology is well-motivated by the theory of Contextual Integrity and demonstrates that context significantly affects human judgments regarding LLM safety, with findings indicating substantial mismatches between human assessments and LLM outputs, particularly in commercial models. The performance of CASE-Bench reveals the necessity of context in safety evaluations, supporting the goal of enhancing user experience while maintaining safety standards.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐的关键需求，以确保其安全部署，强调了现有安全基准的局限性，这些基准往往忽视查询的上下文。以往的方法主要集中在拒绝有问题的查询上，而未考虑上下文因素，导致在安全环境中可能出现误判。提出的CASE-Bench引入了上下文感知安全基准，将上下文纳入安全评估，利用足够数量的注释者来检测基于上下文完整性理论的响应显著差异。该方法揭示了上下文对人类判断LLM安全性的重要影响，研究结果表明人类评估与LLM输出之间存在显著不匹配，尤其是在商业模型中，从而强调了上下文在安全评估中的重要性。
SCARCE: Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings
Authors: Yingjie Wang, Yi Dong, Edmund Lau, Jie Meng, Taylor T Johnson, Xiaowei Huang
First: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 11 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rare events govern the safety profile of modern AI systems, yet their probabilities are extremely difficult to estimate: direct Monte Carlo requires prohibitive sample budgets. Subset Simulation (SS) addresses this by decomposing a rare-event probability into moderate conditional probabilities over nested intermediate events. However, classical SS requires a handcrafted scalar performance function whose sublevel sets define those events, demanding detailed knowledge of the failure geometry and limiting transfer to new domains. We propose SCARCE (Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings), which replaces the performance function with learned latent representations and geometric rulers that score proximity to failure regions. Adaptive thresholding constructs nested intermediate events directly from data. We formalise SCARCE through a non-negative supermartingale, yielding a high-probability upper envelope that remains valid under early stopping. On MNIST misclassification, where dense Monte Carlo provides ground truth, SCARCE achieves approximately 400--500 times lower mean absolute error than grid-searched traditional SS while eliminating systematic over-counting. We then study PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks under a fleet-level threat model with adversarial fraction $η$. On Llama-Guard-3-8B hidden states, a PCA-based ruler attains 2.6% mean relative error for $η\geq 10^{-3}$ against finite-sample references whose average bootstrap relative half-width is 27.9%, and transfers to a GCG-style corpus with 2.93% relative error after recalibration. A directional criterion $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$ ranks rulers consistently with estimation error (Spearman $ρ=0.83$).
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCARCE：通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析
稀有事件主导现代人工智能系统的安全特征，但其概率极难估计：直接的蒙特卡洛方法需要巨大的样本预算。子集模拟（SS）通过将稀有事件概率分解为嵌套中间事件的适度条件概率来解决这个问题。然而，经典的SS需要手工制作的标量性能函数，其子水平集定义这些事件，要求对失效几何形状有详细了解，并限制了向新领域的转移。我们提出了SCARCE（通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析），它用学习的潜在表示和几何尺代替性能函数，以评分接近失效区域。自适应阈值直接从数据构建嵌套中间事件。我们通过非负超马丁戈形式化SCARCE，产生一个在早期停止下仍然有效的高概率上包络。在MNIST错误分类中，稠密的蒙特卡洛提供了真实值，SCARCE的平均绝对误差比网格搜索的传统SS低约400-500倍，同时消除了系统性的过度计数。然后，我们在具有对抗性比例$η$的舰队级威胁模型下研究PAIR风格的LLM越狱。在Llama-Guard-3-8B隐藏状态上，基于PCA的尺在$η\geq 10^{-3}$时对有限样本参考的平均相对误差为2.6%，其平均自助法相对半宽度为27.9%，并在重新校准后转移到GCG风格的语料库，具有2.93%的相对误差。方向标准$\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$与估计误差一致地对尺进行排名（斯皮尔曼$ρ=0.83$）。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of estimating rare event probabilities in AI systems, which are crucial for assessing safety but difficult to quantify using traditional methods like direct Monte Carlo simulations due to their high sample requirements. Existing approaches, such as Subset Simulation (SS), rely on handcrafted performance functions that necessitate extensive knowledge of failure geometries, limiting their applicability to new domains. The proposed method, SCARCE (Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings), innovatively utilizes learned latent representations and geometric rulers to define failure regions, allowing for adaptive thresholding that constructs intermediate events directly from data. This method is formalized through a non-negative supermartingale, providing a reliable upper envelope even with early stopping. In experiments, SCARCE demonstrates a significant improvement in performance, achieving 400-500 times lower mean absolute error than traditional SS on MNIST misclassification tasks, and shows effective application in analyzing PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks, indicating its potential for broader use in rare event characterization.
本研究解决了在人工智能系统中估计稀有事件概率的挑战，这对于评估安全性至关重要，但由于传统方法（如蒙特卡罗和子集模拟（SS））的局限性而难以量化。现有的SS方法需要手工制作的性能函数，这限制了它们在新领域的适用性，并依赖于对故障几何形状的详细了解。所提出的方法SCARCE创新性地用学习的潜在表示和自适应阈值替代了这一性能函数，直接从数据构建中间事件，从而克服了经典SS的局限性。本文的贡献在于通过非负超马丁戈尔形式化SCARCE，提供了即使在早期停止情况下也有效的高概率上界。在实验中，SCARCE在MNIST误分类任务上表现出显著改善，达到比传统SS低400-500倍的平均绝对误差，并有效分析了PAIR风格的LLM越狱，在舰队级威胁模型中显示出2.6%的平均相对误差。
Sparse Autoencoders are Capable LLM Jailbreak Mitigators
Authors: Yannick Assogba, Jacopo Cortellazzi, Javier Abad, Pau Rodriguez, Xavier Suau, Arno Blaas
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-12T21:17:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-27T01:07:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop, ICML 2026. 31 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks remain a persistent threat to large language model safety. We propose Context-Conditioned Delta Steering (CC-Delta), an SAE-based defense that identifies jailbreak-relevant sparse features by comparing token-level representations of the same harmful request with and without jailbreak context. Using paired harmful/jailbreak prompts, CC-Delta selects features via statistical testing and applies inference-time mean-shift steering in SAE latent space. Across four aligned instruction-tuned models and twelve jailbreak attacks, CC-Delta achieves comparable or better safety-utility tradeoffs than baseline defenses operating in dense latent space. In particular, our method clearly outperforms dense mean-shift steering on all four models, and particularly against out-of-distribution attacks, showing that steering in sparse SAE feature space offers advantages over steering in dense activation space for jailbreak mitigation. Our results suggest off-the-shelf SAEs trained for interpretability can be repurposed as practical jailbreak defenses without task-specific training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器能够缓解大型语言模型的越狱攻击
越狱攻击仍然是大型语言模型安全的持续威胁。我们提出了上下文条件下的增量引导（CC-Delta），这是一种基于稀疏自编码器的防御方法，通过比较同一有害请求在有无越狱上下文下的令牌级表示，识别与越狱相关的稀疏特征。使用成对的有害/越狱提示，CC-Delta通过统计测试选择特征，并在稀疏自编码器潜在空间中应用推理时均值偏移引导。在四个对齐的指令调优模型和十二个越狱攻击中，CC-Delta实现了与在稠密潜在空间中运行的基线防御相当或更好的安全效用权衡。特别是，我们的方法在所有四个模型上明显优于稠密均值偏移引导，尤其是在对抗分布外攻击时，表明在稀疏自编码器特征空间中的引导相较于在稠密激活空间中的引导在越狱缓解方面具有优势。我们的结果表明，经过可解释性训练的现成稀疏自编码器可以在不进行特定任务训练的情况下重新用于实际的越狱防御。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the ongoing issue of jailbreak attacks on large language models, which pose significant safety risks. Previous methods primarily relied on dense latent space defenses, which often struggled with effectiveness against various attack types. The proposed Context-Conditioned Delta Steering (CC-Delta) method utilizes Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify relevant features by comparing token-level representations of harmful requests in both jailbreak and non-jailbreak contexts, thus providing a more nuanced approach to defense. This paper contributes a novel defense mechanism that leverages statistical testing for feature selection and applies mean-shift steering in the SAE latent space. The methodology was tested across four instruction-tuned models against twelve jailbreak attacks, demonstrating that CC-Delta achieves comparable or superior safety-utility tradeoffs compared to existing dense defenses, particularly excelling against out-of-distribution attacks, thereby supporting its effectiveness in enhancing model safety.
本研究针对大型语言模型面临的越狱攻击威胁，这种攻击会危害模型的安全性。以往的方法主要依赖于稠密潜在空间的防御，往往在应对此类攻击时效果不佳。提出的上下文条件增量引导（CC-Delta）方法利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）通过比较有无越狱上下文的有害请求的标记级表示来识别相关的稀疏特征，从而克服现有方法的局限性。本文的贡献在于展示了现成的SAE可以有效地重新用于越狱缓解，而无需特定任务的训练。该方法论涉及统计测试以选择特征，并在SAE潜在空间中应用均值偏移引导，在四个对齐的指令调优模型上对十二个越狱攻击实现了可比或更优的安全效用权衡，特别是在分布外场景中表现突出。
Just Ask: Curious Code Agents Reveal System Prompts in Frontier LLMs
Authors: Xiang Zheng, Yutao Wu, Hanxun Huang, Yige Li, Xingjun Ma, Bo Li, Yu-Gang Jiang, Cong Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T03:53:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T10:38:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous code agents built on large language models are reshaping software and AI development through tool use, long-horizon reasoning, and self-directed interaction. However, this autonomy introduces a previously unrecognized security risk: agentic interaction fundamentally expands the LLM attack surface, enabling systematic probing and recovery of hidden system prompts that guide model behavior. We identify system prompt extraction as an emergent vulnerability intrinsic to code agents and present \textbf{\textsc{JustAsk}}, a self-evolving framework that autonomously discovers effective extraction strategies through interaction alone. Unlike prior prompt-engineering or dataset-based attacks, \textsc{JustAsk} requires no handcrafted prompts, labeled supervision, or privileged access beyond standard user interaction. It formulates extraction as an online exploration problem, using Upper Confidence Bound-based strategy selection and a hierarchical skill space spanning atomic probes and high-level orchestration. These skills exploit imperfect system-instruction generalization and inherent tensions between helpfulness and safety. Evaluated on \textbf{41} black-box commercial models across multiple providers, \textsc{JustAsk} consistently achieves full or near-complete system prompt recovery, revealing recurring design- and architecture-level vulnerabilities. Our results expose system prompts as a critical yet largely unprotected attack surface in modern agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：只需询问：好奇的代码代理揭示前沿大型语言模型中的系统提示
基于大型语言模型构建的自主代码代理通过工具使用、长远推理和自我导向交互正在重塑软件和人工智能开发。然而，这种自主性引入了一种之前未被认识的安全风险：代理交互从根本上扩展了大型语言模型的攻击面，使得系统提示的系统性探测和恢复成为可能，这些提示指导模型行为。我们将系统提示提取识别为代码代理内在的新兴漏洞，并提出\textbf{\textsc{JustAsk}}，一个通过交互自主发现有效提取策略的自我演化框架。与之前的提示工程或基于数据集的攻击不同，\textsc{JustAsk}不需要手工提示、标记监督或超出标准用户交互的特权访问。它将提取形式化为在线探索问题，使用基于上置信界的策略选择和跨越原子探测和高级编排的层次技能空间。这些技能利用了系统指令泛化的不完美性以及有用性与安全性之间的固有紧张关系。在对\textbf{41}个来自多个提供商的黑箱商业模型进行评估时，\textsc{JustAsk}始终实现了完整或近乎完整的系统提示恢复，揭示了反复出现的设计和架构级漏洞。我们的结果揭示了系统提示作为现代代理系统中一个关键但在很大程度上未受保护的攻击面。

The research addresses the emerging security risks associated with autonomous code agents built on large language models (LLMs), particularly the vulnerability of system prompt extraction that can be exploited through agentic interactions. Previous methods relied on prompt-engineering or dataset-based attacks, which often required handcrafted prompts or labeled supervision, limiting their effectiveness and accessibility. How Reliable Is Your Jailbreak Judge? Calibration and Adversarial Robustness of Automated ASR Scoring
Authors: Yang Gao
First: 2026-06-24T07:14:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T02:04:55+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables, Code: github.com/gy15901580825/judgeflip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Almost every paper on LLM jailbreaks and prompt injection reports an attack-success rate (ASR), and that number is assigned not by people but by an automated judge: either a safety classifier trained for the task, or a general chat model prompted to grade. The judge is rarely checked. We check it. Using 596 human-labeled completions from the HarmBench classifier validation set, we compare the two judge families against human majority votes and then attack them. The two families fail in opposite ways. The dedicated classifier over-flags (precision 0.835, recall 0.974); three different LLM-as-judges keep high precision (0.81 to 0.94) but show erratic recall (0.06 to 0.65), so the same responses produce very different ASR depending on which judge scores them. The two families also differ sharply in robustness. Wrappers that leave the harmful text untouched and only add benign framing flip every LLM-judge between 57% and 100% of the time, and a single prepended refusal sentence accounts for much of this (39% to 88%). The dedicated classifier resists these surface attacks (at most 6.7%), but a white-box GCG attack on its open weights flips 70% of confident true positives (21 of 30; 95% CI 54 to 86%) even at a small optimization budget. A two-annotator audit confirms the attacks leave the harm intact: every one of 80 sampled flips still contained the harmful content. Because a large and growing share of reported ASR comes from LLM-judges, many such numbers are unreliable both on average and under deliberate pressure. We recommend that papers report judge precision and recall on a human-labeled slice, report ASR corrected for judge precision, and include an adversarial check of the judge. Our code is released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的越狱评估器有多可靠？自动化ASR评分的校准与对抗鲁棒性
几乎每篇关于LLM越狱和提示注入的论文都报告了攻击成功率（ASR），而这个数字不是由人类赋予的，而是由一个自动评估器给出的：要么是为此任务训练的安全分类器，要么是被提示进行评分的一般聊天模型。评估器很少被检查。我们进行了检查。使用来自HarmBench分类器验证集的596个人工标注的完成结果，我们将这两种评估器与人类多数投票进行比较，然后对其进行攻击。这两种评估器在失败方式上截然相反。专用分类器过度标记（精确度0.835，召回率0.974）；三种不同的LLM作为评估者保持高精度（0.81到0.94），但显示出不稳定的召回率（0.06到0.65），因此相同的响应根据哪个评估器评分会产生非常不同的ASR。这两种评估器在鲁棒性上也有显著差异。那些不改变有害文本，仅添加良性框架的包装器使每个LLM评估者在57%到100%的时间内翻转，而一个单独的前置拒绝句子占据了其中很大一部分（39%到88%）。专用分类器抵御这些表面攻击（最多6.7%），但对其开放权重的白盒GCG攻击在小的优化预算下翻转了70%的自信真阳性（30个中的21个；95%置信区间54到86%）。两位标注者的审计确认攻击保持了有害内容的完整性：80个抽样翻转中的每一个仍然包含有害内容。由于越来越多的报告ASR来自LLM评估者，许多这样的数字在平均值和故意压力下都是不可靠的。我们建议论文报告人类标注片段上的评估器精度和召回率，报告经过评估器精度修正的ASR，并包括对评估器的对抗检查。我们的代码已发布。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the reliability of automated judges used to report attack-success rates (ASR) in studies on LLM jailbreaks and prompt injection, highlighting that these judges are often unchecked. Previous methods relied on either dedicated safety classifiers or general chat models, which exhibited significant flaws in precision and recall. The proposed approach systematically evaluates these judges against human majority votes and tests their robustness against adversarial attacks, revealing that dedicated classifiers over-flag while LLM judges show erratic recall. The paper contributes by demonstrating the unreliability of reported ASR due to these judge inconsistencies and recommends improved reporting standards for ASR metrics. The methodology involves comparing human-labeled completions with automated judgments and conducting adversarial tests, achieving insights that underscore the need for more reliable evaluation methods in this domain.

RAS: Measuring LLM Safety Through Refusal Alignment
Authors: Chang-Chieh Huang, Yan-Lun Chen, Chia-Mu Yu, Wei-Bin Lee
First: 2026-06-24T12:19:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T12:19:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is commonly performed by querying models with unsafe or jailbreak prompts and judging whether their outputs violate a safety policy. Although useful, output-level evaluation is expensive, sensitive to judge choice, and easily tied to fixed question banks. We propose **SafeVec**, a white-box evaluation procedure that measures safety from internal representations rather than generated answers. **SafeVec** first extracts layer-wise refusal directions from a safety-aligned reference model, then selects stable layer windows where safe and unsafe behaviors are separable, and finally scores a target model by measuring whether its hidden states align with these refusal directions under unsafe and jailbreak prompts. The resulting metric, **RAS** (**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core), maps representation-level refusal alignment to a calibrated 0-100 safety score. Across `Llama`, `Gemma`, and `Qwen` model families, RAS separates aligned models from uncensored and abliterated variants, tracks output-level attack success rate, and is substantially faster than judge-based evaluation. These results suggest that refusal alignment provides a compact and efficient signal for white-box LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAS：通过拒绝对齐测量大型语言模型的安全性
大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全性评估通常通过使用不安全或越狱提示查询模型，并判断其输出是否违反安全政策。尽管有用，输出级评估成本高、对评判者选择敏感，并且容易与固定问题库挂钩。我们提出了**SafeVec**，一种白盒评估程序，从内部表示而非生成的答案来测量安全性。**SafeVec**首先从安全对齐的参考模型中提取逐层拒绝方向，然后选择安全和不安全行为可分离的稳定层窗口，最后通过测量目标模型的隐藏状态在不安全和越狱提示下是否与这些拒绝方向对齐来评分。最终指标**RAS**（**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core）将表示级拒绝对齐映射到校准的0-100安全分数。在`Llama`、`Gemma`和`Qwen`模型系列中，RAS将对齐模型与未审查和被消除的变体区分开，跟踪输出级攻击成功率，并且比基于评判者的评估快得多。这些结果表明，拒绝对齐为白盒LLM安全评估提供了紧凑而高效的信号。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of evaluating the safety of large language models (LLMs), which has traditionally relied on output-level assessments that are costly, subjective, and dependent on fixed question banks. Previous methods often struggle with these limitations, prompting the authors to propose a novel approach called SafeVec, which evaluates safety based on internal representations rather than generated outputs. This method is well-motivated as it aims to provide a more efficient and objective measure of safety. The paper introduces the Refusal Alignment Score (RAS), which quantifies the alignment of a model's hidden states with safety-aligned refusal directions extracted from a reference model. The methodology demonstrates that RAS effectively distinguishes between aligned and uncensored models across various model families, showing a strong correlation with output-level attack success rates while being significantly faster than traditional judge-based evaluations, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM safety assessment.

FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T07:42:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinRED：一个专家指导的金融LLM红队评估基准生成框架
现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规性违规、欺诈促进和系统性信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入了FinRED，这是一个由金融专家开发的专家指导的金融LLM安全评估红队框架。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从监管规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认了种子的合理性和真实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键的假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，访问链接为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the gap in existing safety benchmarks for financial large language models (LLMs), which often overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud. Previous methods have focused on general adversarial scenarios, leading to inadequate evaluations for financial contexts. The proposed FinRED framework, developed with input from financial experts, introduces a two-level taxonomy that maps global standards to specific financial threats, enhancing the evaluation process. The methodology includes a scalable pipeline that transforms real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming prompts, validated by experts to ensure their plausibility. FinRED significantly improves evaluation accuracy by reducing critical false negatives from 28 to 12 and is aligned with international risk-management standards, demonstrating its effectiveness in a regulatory sandbox in South Korea for generative AI security evaluation in financial services.
本文解决了现有金融大型语言模型（LLM）安全基准的不足，这些基准往往忽视了诸如合规违规和欺诈等金融特定风险。以往的方法主要集中于一般对抗场景，未能提供金融领域所需的针对性评估。所提出的FinRED框架在金融专家的指导下开发，引入了一个将全球标准映射到特定威胁的两级分类法，并利用可扩展的管道从真实金融文档中创建上下文丰富的红队提示。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它增强了评估场景的现实性和可信度，显著减少了评估中的关键假阴性。该方法包括专家验证和与人类专家评估紧密对齐的金融特定评分标准。FinRED已在韩国金融安全研究所的监管沙箱中部署，证明其在改善金融服务中生成性人工智能的安全评估方面的有效性。
TROPT: An Open Framework for Unifying and Advancing Discrete Text Optimization
Authors: Matan Ben-Tov, Mahmood Sharif
First: 2026-06-22T15:44:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T15:44:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete text-trigger optimization -- searching for text sequences that, when ingested by a model, steer it toward a specified objective -- underpins model red-teaming (e.g., LLM jailbreaks), as well as auditing and interpretability. However, the current state of discrete optimizers hinders their adoption and progress. First, existing optimizers, when open-sourced at all, are scattered across research codebases tied to specific models, objectives, and problem domains. Second, optimizer variants proliferate, each requiring engineering overhead to use or extend, and remaining hard to compare head-to-head. Together, these raise the bar for adopting optimizers in existing or new domains, and for advancing them via new strategies. We address these gaps with TROPT, the first open-source framework that unifies discrete optimizers' execution and standardizes their development under a single interface. TROPT makes it easy to customize end-to-end optimization recipes by swapping any component -- models, objectives, and optimizers -- extending its reach across domains and new applications. TROPT currently ships with 30+ optimization recipes -- covering applications such as jailbreaking and probing model internals -- built from 15+ optimizers (spanning white-box to black-box access) and 15+ losses, from foundational to state-of-the-art methods. Demonstrating its utility, we leverage TROPT in several studies: (i) controlled, large-scale experiments comparing and enhancing optimization strategies for LLM jailbreaks, revealing potent-yet-underadopted techniques; and (ii) porting optimizers from one domain (e.g., LLM jailbreak) to new domains (e.g., corpus-poisoning embedding model). In all, TROPT significantly lowers the barrier to adopting and advancing discrete text optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TROPT：一个统一和推进离散文本优化的开放框架
离散文本触发优化——寻找文本序列，当被模型摄取时，引导其朝向特定目标——是模型红队（例如，LLM越狱）以及审计和可解释性的基础。然而，当前离散优化器的状态阻碍了它们的采用和进展。首先，现有的优化器如果开源，通常分散在与特定模型、目标和问题领域相关的研究代码库中。其次，优化器变体繁多，每个都需要工程开销来使用或扩展，并且难以进行直接比较。这些因素共同提高了在现有或新领域中采用优化器的门槛，并通过新策略推进它们。我们通过TROPT解决这些问题，这是第一个统一离散优化器执行并在单一接口下标准化其开发的开源框架。TROPT使得通过更换任何组件——模型、目标和优化器——轻松定制端到端优化配方，扩展其在不同领域和新应用中的适用性。TROPT目前提供30多个优化配方——涵盖越狱和探测模型内部等应用——由15个以上的优化器（从白盒到黑盒访问）和15个以上的损失函数（从基础到最先进的方法）构建。为了展示其效用，我们在几项研究中利用TROPT：（i）控制的大规模实验比较和增强LLM越狱的优化策略，揭示出强大但未被广泛采用的技术；（ii）将优化器从一个领域（例如，LLM越狱）移植到新领域（例如，语料库中毒嵌入模型）。总之，TROPT显著降低了采用和推进离散文本优化的门槛。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current discrete text-trigger optimization methods, which are often fragmented across various codebases and difficult to compare, hindering their adoption and advancement. Existing methods suffer from a lack of standardization and high engineering overhead, making it challenging to implement or extend them for new applications. The proposed solution, TROPT, is an open-source framework that unifies the execution of discrete optimizers and standardizes their development under a single interface, facilitating easier customization and application across different domains. The framework includes over 30 optimization recipes derived from more than 15 optimizers and losses, demonstrating its versatility in tasks such as LLM jailbreaks and corpus-poisoning. The experimental results indicate that TROPT effectively enhances optimization strategies and lowers barriers for researchers and practitioners in the field.

Culturally-Adapted Red-Teaming Across East and Southeast Asian Contexts: A Methodological and Comparative Analysis
Authors: Hyeji Choi, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-08T08:17:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T07:46:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 Workshop on Culture X AI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) has predominantly relied on direct translation (DT) of English benchmarks into target languages - an approach that converts surface-level linguistic form while failing to reflect the cultural context embedded in threat scenarios, social norms, and legal frameworks. We construct paired DT and culturally-adapted (CA) datasets via 1:1 seed matching for four languages - Korean (KO), Japanese (JA), Thai (TH), and Khmer (KM) - and compare Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Cultural Realism scores across four open-source LLM. CA prompts yield Delta-ASR > 0 across all 16 language x model combinations (mean +9.3 pp), and DT-based evaluation underestimates risk in 44 of 48 category x language combinations. Language-level analysis reveals that the distribution of threat forms is heterogeneous across languages. Cultural Realism analysis further shows that DT Cultural Depth (C3) scores remain consistently below 1.0 out of 3.0 across all four languages (mean 0.17), whereas CA scores reach up to 2.51, indicating that direct translation produces inputs systematically divergent from those encountered in real-world multicultural settings. These findings demonstrate that adapting benchmarks to language-specific cultural contexts - rather than relying on linguistic translation alone - is necessary for valid multilingual LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨东亚和东南亚背景的文化适应性红队：方法论和比较分析
大型语言模型（LLMs）的多语言安全评估主要依赖于将英语基准直接翻译（DT）为目标语言，这种方法仅转换表层语言形式，未能反映威胁场景、社会规范和法律框架中嵌入的文化背景。我们通过1:1种子匹配构建了四种语言（韩语、日语、泰语和高棉语）的配对DT和文化适应（CA）数据集，并比较了四个开源LLM的攻击成功率（ASR）和文化现实评分。CA提示在所有16种语言x模型组合中均产生Delta-ASR > 0（平均+9.3个百分点），而基于DT的评估在48个类别x语言组合中低估了风险。语言级分析显示，威胁形式的分布在不同语言中是异质的。文化现实分析进一步表明，DT文化深度（C3）评分在所有四种语言中始终低于3.0的1.0（平均0.17），而CA评分高达2.51，表明直接翻译产生的输入与现实世界多文化环境中遇到的输入系统性地不同。这些发现表明，适应特定语言文化背景的基准，而不是仅依赖语言翻译，对于有效的多语言LLM安全评估是必要的。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing multilingual safety evaluations of large language models (LLMs), which have primarily depended on direct translation of English benchmarks, failing to account for the cultural contexts inherent in threat scenarios. Previous methods inadequately reflect social norms and legal frameworks, leading to an underestimation of risks in various languages. The proposed approach introduces culturally-adapted (CA) datasets alongside direct translation (DT) datasets for Korean, Japanese, Thai, and Khmer, allowing for a more nuanced evaluation of LLMs. This methodology involves constructing paired datasets through 1:1 seed matching and comparing Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Cultural Realism scores across four open-source LLMs. The results indicate that CA prompts significantly improve ASR, with a mean increase of 9.3 percentage points, and reveal that DT evaluations underestimate risks in 44 out of 48 combinations, highlighting the necessity of culturally-informed benchmarks for accurate multilingual safety assessments.
